### 6. **Test Patterns & Infrastructure**

- Null value stripping and metadata filtering

- Supports stdio, SSE, and Streamable HTTP transports

### 8. **Tool Registration & Agent Loop Integration**

**Tool Registration Flow:**
- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/zesty-variety/crates/mcp-agent-bridge/src/adapter.rs`:

- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/zesty-variety/crates/sessions/migrations/20260205140000_session_mcp_disabled.sql` - Database migration for MCP session handling
- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/zesty-variety/crates/web/src/assets/js/` - JavaScript UI for MCP management (page-mcp.js, mcp-store.js, locales)

### Summary

The codebase has **comprehensive test infrastructure**:
- **52+ Rust unit tests** using tokio-test, mockito, and tempfile
- **~13 integration tests** in the agent-bridge adapter
- **11 E2E test cases** with Playwright covering UI workflows

     1→//! MCP health polling and auto-restart background task.
     2→
     3→use std::{
     4→    collections::HashMap,
     5→    sync::Arc,
     6→    time::{Duration, Instant},
     7→};
     8→
     9→use tracing::{info, warn};
    10→
    11→use crate::{
    12→    broadcast::{BroadcastOpts, broadcast},
    13→    mcp_service::LiveMcpService,
    14→    state::GatewayState,
    15→};
    16→
    17→const POLL_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);
    18→const MAX_RESTART_ATTEMPTS: u32 = 5;
    19→const BASE_BACKOFF: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);
    20→const MAX_BACKOFF: Duration = Duration::from_secs(300);
    21→
    22→struct RestartState {
    23→    count: u32,
    24→    last_attempt: Instant,
    25→}
    26→
    27→/// Run the health monitor loop. Checks all MCP servers periodically,
    28→/// broadcasts status changes, and auto-restarts dead servers with backoff.
    29→pub async fn run_health_monitor(state: Arc<GatewayState>, mcp: Arc<LiveMcpService>) {
    30→    let mut prev_states: HashMap<String, String> = HashMap::new();
    31→    let mut restart_states: HashMap<String, RestartState> = HashMap::new();
    32→
    33→    loop {
    34→        tokio::time::sleep(POLL_INTERVAL).await;
    35→
    36→        let statuses = mcp.manager().status_all().await;
    37→
    38→        let mut changed = false;
    39→        for s in &statuses {
    40→            let prev = prev_states.get(&s.name).map(String::as_str);
    41→            if prev != Some(&s.state) {
    42→                changed = true;
    43→
    44→                // Auto-restart: if a server was previously running and is now dead.
    45→                // Skip restart if the server is in the middle of OAuth authentication.
    46→                let awaiting_auth = s.auth_state == Some(moltis_mcp::McpAuthState::AwaitingBrowser);
    47→                if prev == Some("running") && s.state == "dead" && s.enabled && !awaiting_auth {
    48→                    let rs = restart_states
    49→                        .entry(s.name.clone())
    50→                        .or_insert(RestartState {
    51→                            count: 0,
    52→                            last_attempt: Instant::now() - MAX_BACKOFF,
    53→                        });
    54→
    55→                    if rs.count < MAX_RESTART_ATTEMPTS {
    56→                        let backoff = std::cmp::min(
    57→                            BASE_BACKOFF * 2u32.saturating_pow(rs.count),
    58→                            MAX_BACKOFF,
    59→                        );
    60→                        if rs.last_attempt.elapsed() >= backoff {
    61→                            info!(
    62→                                server = %s.name,
    63→                                attempt = rs.count + 1,
    64→                                "auto-restarting dead MCP server"
    65→                            );
    66→                            rs.count += 1;
    67→                            rs.last_attempt = Instant::now();
    68→
    69→                            match mcp.manager().restart_server(&s.name).await {
    70→                                Ok(()) => {
    71→                                    mcp.sync_tools_if_ready().await;
    72→                                    info!(server = %s.name, "MCP server auto-restarted");
    73→                                },
    74→                                Err(e) => {
    75→                                    warn!(
    76→                                        server = %s.name,
    77→                                        error = %e,
    78→                                        "MCP auto-restart failed"
    79→                                    );
    80→                                },
    81→                            }
    82→                        }
    83→                    } else if rs.count == MAX_RESTART_ATTEMPTS {
    84→                        warn!(
    85→                            server = %s.name,
    86→                            "MCP server exceeded max restart attempts, giving up"
    87→                        );
    88→                        rs.count += 1; // prevent repeating this warning
    89→                    }
    90→                }
    91→
    92→                // Reset restart counter when a server comes back to running
    93→                if s.state == "running" {
    94→                    restart_states.remove(&s.name);
    95→                }
    96→            }
    97→            prev_states.insert(s.name.clone(), s.state.clone());
    98→        }
    99→
   100→        // Remove entries for servers no longer in the registry
   101→        prev_states.retain(|name, _| statuses.iter().any(|s| &s.name == name));
   102→        restart_states.retain(|name, _| statuses.iter().any(|s| &s.name == name));
   103→
   104→        if changed {
   105→            let payload = serde_json::to_value(&statuses).unwrap_or_default();
   106→            broadcast(&state, "mcp.status", payload, BroadcastOpts::default()).await;
   107→        }
   108→    }
   109→}
   110→
   111→#[cfg(test)]
   112→mod tests {
   113→    use super::*;
   114→
   115→    #[test]
   116→    fn test_backoff_calculation() {
   117→        // Verify backoff growth: 5, 10, 20, 40, 80 (capped at 300)
   118→        for i in 0..MAX_RESTART_ATTEMPTS {
   119→            let backoff = std::cmp::min(BASE_BACKOFF * 2u32.saturating_pow(i), MAX_BACKOFF);
   120→            assert!(backoff >= BASE_BACKOFF);
   121→            assert!(backoff <= MAX_BACKOFF);
   122→        }
   123→    }
   124→
   125→    #[test]
   126→    fn test_max_backoff_cap() {
   127→        let backoff = std::cmp::min(BASE_BACKOFF * 2u32.saturating_pow(10), MAX_BACKOFF);
   128→        assert_eq!(backoff, MAX_BACKOFF);
   129→    }
   130→}
   131→

    27→/// Live MCP service delegating to `McpManager`.
    28→pub struct LiveMcpService {
    29→    manager: Arc<moltis_mcp::McpManager>,
    30→    /// Shared tool registry for syncing MCP tools into the agent loop.
    31→    /// Set after construction via `set_tool_registry`.
    32→    tool_registry: RwLock<Option<Arc<RwLock<ToolRegistry>>>>,
    33→    config_env_overrides: HashMap<String, String>,
    34→    credential_store: RwLock<Option<Arc<crate::auth::CredentialStore>>>,
    35→}
    36→
    37→impl LiveMcpService {
    38→    pub fn new(
    39→        manager: Arc<moltis_mcp::McpManager>,
    40→        config_env_overrides: HashMap<String, String>,
    41→        credential_store: Option<Arc<crate::auth::CredentialStore>>,
    42→    ) -> Self {
    43→        Self {
    44→            manager,
    45→            tool_registry: RwLock::new(None),
    46→            config_env_overrides,
    47→            credential_store: RwLock::new(credential_store),
    48→        }
    49→    }
    50→
    51→    /// Store a reference to the shared tool registry so MCP mutations
    52→    /// can automatically sync tools.
    53→    pub async fn set_tool_registry(&self, registry: Arc<RwLock<ToolRegistry>>) {
    54→        *self.tool_registry.write().await = Some(registry);
    55→    }
    56→
    57→    /// Sync MCP tools into the shared tool registry (if set).
    58→    pub async fn sync_tools_if_ready(&self) {
    59→        let maybe_reg = self.tool_registry.read().await.clone();
    60→        if let Some(reg) = maybe_reg {
    61→            sync_mcp_tools(&self.manager, &reg).await;
    62→        }
    63→    }
    64→
    65→    /// Access the underlying manager.
    66→    pub fn manager(&self) -> &Arc<moltis_mcp::McpManager> {
    67→        &self.manager
    68→    }
    69→
    70→    pub async fn set_credential_store(&self, credential_store: Arc<crate::auth::CredentialStore>) {
    71→        *self.credential_store.write().await = Some(credential_store);
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    async fn refresh_manager_env_overrides(&self) {
    75→        let credential_store = self.credential_store.read().await.clone();
    76→        let env_overrides = if let Some(store) = credential_store {
    77→            match store.get_all_env_values().await {
    78→                Ok(db_env_vars) => merge_env_overrides(&self.config_env_overrides, db_env_vars),
    79→                Err(error) => {
    80→                    warn!(%error, "failed to refresh MCP env overrides from credential store");
    81→                    self.config_env_overrides.clone()
    82→                },
    83→            }
    84→        } else {
    85→            self.config_env_overrides.clone()
    86→        };
    87→
    88→        self.manager.set_env_overrides(env_overrides).await;
    89→    }
    90→}
    91→
    92→#[async_trait]
    93→impl McpService for LiveMcpService {
    94→    async fn list(&self) -> ServiceResult {
    95→        let statuses = self.manager.status_all().await;
    96→        Ok(serde_json::to_value(&statuses)?)
    97→    }
    98→
    99→    async fn add(&self, params: Value) -> ServiceResult {
   100→        let name = params
   101→            .get("name")
   102→            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
   103→            .ok_or_else(|| "missing 'name' parameter".to_string())?;
   104→        let redirect_uri = params
   105→            .get("redirectUri")
   106→            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
   107→            .map(str::trim)
   108→            .filter(|v| !v.is_empty())
   109→            .map(ToOwned::to_owned);
   110→        let config =
   111→            parse_server_config(&params, None).map_err(|e| ServiceError::message(e.to_string()))?;
   112→        self.refresh_manager_env_overrides().await;
   113→
   114→        // If a server with this name already exists, append a numeric suffix.
   115→        let final_name = {
   116→            let reg = self.manager.registry_snapshot().await;
   117→            let mut candidate = name.to_string();
   118→            let mut n = 2u32;
   119→            while reg.servers.contains_key(&candidate) {
   120→                candidate = format!("{name}-{n}");
   121→                n += 1;
   122→            }
   123→            candidate
   124→        };
   125→
   126→        info!(server = %final_name, "adding MCP server via API");
   127→        match self
   128→            .manager
   129→            .add_server(final_name.clone(), config, true)
   130→            .await
   131→        {
   132→            Ok(_) => {
   133→                self.sync_tools_if_ready().await;
   134→                Ok(serde_json::json!({ "ok": true, "name": final_name }))
   135→            },
   136→            Err(e) => {
   137→                if matches!(
   138→                    e,
   139→                    moltis_mcp::Error::Manager(moltis_mcp::McpManagerError::OAuthRequired { .. })
   140→                ) {
   141→                    if let Some(uri) = redirect_uri {
   142→                        let auth_url = self
   143→                            .manager
   144→                            .oauth_start_server(&final_name, &uri)
   145→                            .await
   146→                            .map_err(ServiceError::message)?;
   147→                        Ok(serde_json::json!({
   148→                            "ok": true,
   149→                            "name": final_name,
   150→                            "oauthPending": true,
   151→                            "authUrl": auth_url
   152→                        }))
   153→                    } else {
   154→                        Ok(serde_json::json!({
   155→                            "ok": true,
   156→                            "name": final_name,
   157→                            "oauthPending": true
   158→                        }))
   159→                    }
   160→                } else {
   161→                    Err(ServiceError::message(e))
   162→                }
   163→            },
   164→        }
   165→    }

   173→        let removed = self

     1→// Mock OAuth server for E2E testing.
     2→// Implements /authorize (PKCE + state), /token (code exchange + refresh), and /calls (request log).
     3→// Usage: node mock-oauth-server.js
     4→// Prints JSON to stdout: { "port": <number> }
     5→
     6→const http = require("node:http");
     7→const crypto = require("node:crypto");
     8→
     9→var calls = [];
    10→// Map of state -> { challenge, redirectUri }
    11→var pendingFlows = new Map();
    12→// Tracks issued auth codes -> { state } so /token can verify
    13→var issuedCodes = new Map();
    14→// Whether /token should return errors (toggled via /config)
    15→var tokenShouldFail = false;
    16→// Whether /authorize should avoid redirecting to callback (for manual paste tests)
    17→var authorizeShouldNotRedirect = false;
    18→// Last generated callback URL from /authorize
    19→var lastRedirectUrl = null;
    20→
    21→function parseRequestUrl(req) {
    22→	return new URL(req.url, "http://127.0.0.1");
    23→}
    24→
    25→function queryObject(searchParams) {
    26→	var query = {};
    27→	for (const [key, value] of searchParams.entries()) {
    28→		query[key] = value;
    29→	}
    30→	return query;
    31→}
    32→
    33→function base64UrlEncode(buffer) {
    34→	return buffer.toString("base64").replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
    35→}
    36→
    37→function verifyPkce(verifier, challenge) {
    38→	var hash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(verifier).digest();
    39→	var expected = base64UrlEncode(hash);
    40→	return expected === challenge;
    41→}
    42→
    43→function parseBody(req) {
    44→	return new Promise((resolve) => {
    45→		var chunks = [];
    46→		req.on("data", (c) => chunks.push(c));
    47→		req.on("end", () => {
    48→			var body = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString();
    49→			resolve(new URLSearchParams(body));
    50→		});
    51→	});
    52→}
    53→
    54→function respond(res, status, body) {
    55→	var json = JSON.stringify(body);
    56→	res.writeHead(status, { "Content-Type": "application/json" });
    57→	res.end(json);
    58→}
    59→
    60→function handleAuthorize(res, query) {
    61→	if (!(query.client_id && query.redirect_uri && query.code_challenge && query.state)) {
    62→		return respond(res, 400, {
    63→			error: "invalid_request",
    64→			error_description: "Missing required parameters: client_id, redirect_uri, code_challenge, state",
    65→		});
    66→	}
    67→	if (query.code_challenge_method !== "S256") {
    68→		return respond(res, 400, {
    69→			error: "invalid_request",
    70→			error_description: "Only S256 code_challenge_method is supported",
    71→		});
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	pendingFlows.set(query.state, {
    75→		challenge: query.code_challenge,
    76→		redirectUri: query.redirect_uri,
    77→	});
    78→
    79→	var authCode = `mock-auth-code-${crypto.randomBytes(8).toString("hex")}`;
    80→	issuedCodes.set(authCode, { state: query.state });
    81→
    82→	var redirectUrl = new URL(query.redirect_uri);
    83→	redirectUrl.searchParams.set("code", authCode);
    84→	redirectUrl.searchParams.set("state", query.state);
    85→	lastRedirectUrl = redirectUrl.toString();
    86→
    87→	if (authorizeShouldNotRedirect) {
    88→		return respond(res, 200, {
    89→			ok: true,
    90→			note: "authorize_should_not_redirect is enabled",
    91→			redirect_url: lastRedirectUrl,
    92→		});
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	res.writeHead(302, { Location: redirectUrl.toString() });
    96→	res.end();
    97→}
    98→
    99→function handleTokenExchange(res, body) {
   100→	var authCode = body.get("code");
   101→	var verifier = body.get("code_verifier");
   102→	var clientId = body.get("client_id");
   103→
   104→	if (!(authCode && verifier && clientId)) {
   105→		return respond(res, 400, {
   106→			error: "invalid_request",
   107→			error_description: "Missing code, code_verifier, or client_id",
   108→		});
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	var codeEntry = issuedCodes.get(authCode);
   112→	if (!codeEntry) {
   113→		return respond(res, 400, {
   114→			error: "invalid_grant",
   115→			error_description: "Unknown or expired authorization code",
   116→		});
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	var flow = pendingFlows.get(codeEntry.state);
   120→	if (!flow) {
   121→		return respond(res, 400, {
   122→			error: "invalid_grant",
   123→			error_description: "No pending flow for this state",
   124→		});
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	if (!verifyPkce(verifier, flow.challenge)) {
   128→		return respond(res, 400, {
   129→			error: "invalid_grant",
   130→			error_description: "PKCE verification failed",
   131→		});
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	issuedCodes.delete(authCode);
   135→	pendingFlows.delete(codeEntry.state);
   136→
   137→	return respond(res, 200, {
   138→		access_token=[REDACTED]${crypto.randomBytes(8).toString("hex")}`,
   139→		refresh_token=[REDACTED]${crypto.randomBytes(8).toString("hex")}`,
   140→		token_type: "Bearer",
   141→		expires_in: 3600,
   142→	});
   143→}
   144→
   145→function handleTokenRefresh(res, body) {
   146→	var refreshToken = body.get("refresh_token");
   147→	if (!refreshToken) {
   148→		return respond(res, 400, {
   149→			error: "invalid_request",
   150→			error_description: "Missing refresh_token",
   151→		});
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	return respond(res, 200, {
   155→		access_token=[REDACTED]${crypto.randomBytes(8).toString("hex")}`,
   156→		refresh_token=[REDACTED]${crypto.randomBytes(8).toString("hex")}`,
   157→		token_type: "Bearer",
   158→		expires_in: 3600,
   159→	});
   160→}
   161→
   162→async function handleToken(res, req) {
   163→	var body = await parseBody(req);
   164→	var grantType = body.get("grant_type");
   165→
   166→	if (tokenShouldFail) {
   167→		return respond(res, 400, {
   168→			error: "server_error",
   169→			error_description: "Mock server configured to return errors",
   170→		});
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	if (grantType === "authorization_code") {
   174→		return handleTokenExchange(res, body);
   175→	}
   176→	if (grantType === "refresh_token") {
   177→		return handleTokenRefresh(res, body);
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	return respond(res, 400, {
   181→		error: "unsupported_grant_type",
   182→		error_description: `Unsupported grant_type: ${grantType}`,
   183→	});
   184→}
   185→
   186→async function handleConfig(req) {
   187→	var configBody = await parseBody(req);
   188→	if (configBody.has("token_should_fail")) {
   189→		tokenShouldFail = configBody.get("token_should_fail") === "true";
   190→	}
   191→	if (configBody.has("authorize_should_not_redirect")) {
   192→		authorizeShouldNotRedirect = configBody.get("authorize_should_not_redirect") === "true";
   193→	}
   194→}
   195→
   196→function handleReset() {
   197→	calls = [];
   198→	pendingFlows.clear();
   199→	issuedCodes.clear();
   200→	tokenShouldFail = false;
   201→	authorizeShouldNotRedirect = false;
   202→	lastRedirectUrl = null;
   203→}
   204→
   205→var server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
   206→	var parsed = parseRequestUrl(req);
   207→	var pathname = parsed.pathname;
   208→	var query = queryObject(parsed.searchParams);
   209→
   210→	calls.push({
   211→		method: req.method,
   212→		path: pathname,
   213→		query,
   214→		timestamp: Date.now(),
   215→	});
   216→
   217→	if (req.method === "GET" && pathname === "/authorize") {
   218→		return handleAuthorize(res, query);
   219→	}
   220→	if (req.method === "POST" && pathname === "/token") {
   221→		return handleToken(res, req);
   222→	}
   223→	if (req.method === "GET" && pathname === "/calls") {
   224→		return respond(res, 200, calls);
   225→	}
   226→	if (req.method === "GET" && pathname === "/last-redirect") {
   227→		return respond(res, 200, { redirect_url: lastRedirectUrl });
   228→	}
   229→	if (req.method === "POST" && pathname === "/config") {
   230→		await handleConfig(req);
   231→		return respond(res, 200, { ok: true });
   232→	}
   233→	if (req.method === "POST" && pathname === "/reset") {
   234→		handleReset();
   235→		return respond(res, 200, { ok: true });
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	respond(res, 404, { error: "not_found" });
   239→});
   240→
   241→server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", () => {
   242→	var port = server.address().port;
   243→	process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ port })}\n`);
   244→});
   245→

    29→/// Status of a managed MCP server.
    30→#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize)]
    31→pub struct ServerStatus {
    32→    pub name: String,
    33→    pub state: String,
    34→    pub enabled: bool,
    35→    pub tool_count: usize,
    36→    pub server_info: Option<String>,

    40→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]

    42→    pub configured_request_timeout_secs: u64,
    43→    pub transport: crate::registry::TransportType,
    44→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]

    46→    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]

    48→    /// OAuth authentication state (only for SSE servers with auth).
    49→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    50→    pub auth_state: Option<McpAuthState>,
    51→    /// Pending OAuth URL to open in browser (when auth_state is awaiting_browser).
    52→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]

    54→    /// Custom display name for the server (shown in UI).

    56→    pub display_name: Option<String>,
    57→}
    58→
    59→/// Mutable state behind the single `RwLock` on [`McpManager`].
    60→pub struct McpManagerInner {
    61→    pub clients: HashMap<String, Arc<RwLock<dyn McpClientTrait>>>,
    62→    pub tools: HashMap<String, Vec<McpToolDef>>,
    63→    pub registry: McpRegistry,
    64→    /// OAuth auth providers for SSE servers, keyed by server name.
    65→    pub auth_providers: HashMap<String, SharedAuthProvider>,
    66→    pub env_overrides: HashMap<String, String>,
    67→}
    68→
    69→/// Manages the lifecycle of multiple MCP server connections.
    70→pub struct McpManager {
    71→    pub inner: RwLock<McpManagerInner>,
    72→    request_timeout_secs: AtomicU64,
    73→}
    74→
    75→impl McpManager {
    76→    pub fn new(registry: McpRegistry) -> Self {
    77→        Self::new_with_env_overrides(registry, HashMap::new(), Duration::from_secs(30))
    78→    }
    79→
    80→    pub fn new_with_request_timeout(registry: McpRegistry, request_timeout: Duration) -> Self {
    81→        Self::new_with_env_overrides(registry, HashMap::new(), request_timeout)
    82→    }
    83→
    84→    pub fn new_with_env_overrides(
    85→        registry: McpRegistry,
    86→        env_overrides: HashMap<String, String>,
    87→        request_timeout: Duration,
    88→    ) -> Self {
    89→        let request_timeout_secs = request_timeout.as_secs().max(1);
    90→        Self {
    91→            inner: RwLock::new(McpManagerInner {
    92→                clients: HashMap::new(),
    93→                tools: HashMap::new(),
    94→                registry,
    95→                auth_providers: HashMap::new(),
    96→                env_overrides,
    97→            }),
    98→            request_timeout_secs: AtomicU64::new(request_timeout_secs),
    99→        }
   100→    }
   101→
   102→    pub async fn set_env_overrides(&self, env_overrides: HashMap<String, String>) {
   103→        self.inner.write().await.env_overrides = env_overrides;
   104→    }
   105→
   106→    pub fn set_request_timeout_secs(&self, request_timeout_secs: u64) {
   107→        self.request_timeout_secs
   108→            .store(request_timeout_secs.max(1), Ordering::Relaxed);
   109→    }
   110→
   111→    fn default_request_timeout_secs(&self) -> u64 {
   112→        self.request_timeout_secs.load(Ordering::Relaxed).max(1)
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    fn effective_timeout_for(&self, config: &McpServerConfig) -> Duration {
   116→        Duration::from_secs(
   117→            config
   118→                .request_timeout_secs
   119→                .filter(|secs| *secs > 0)
   120→                .unwrap_or(self.default_request_timeout_secs()),
   121→        )
   122→    }
   123→
   124→    fn effective_timeout_secs_for(&self, config: &McpServerConfig) -> u64 {
   125→        self.effective_timeout_for(config).as_secs()
   126→    }
   127→
   128→    fn build_auth_provider(
   129→        name: &str,
   130→        remote: &ResolvedRemoteConfig,
   131→        oauth: Option<&McpOAuthConfig>,
   132→    ) -> SharedAuthProvider {
   133→        let provider = if let Some(ov) = oauth {
   134→            McpOAuthProvider::new(name, remote.request_url()).with_oauth_override(
   135→                McpOAuthOverride {
   136→                    client_id: ov.client_id.clone(),
   137→                    auth_url: ov.auth_url.clone(),
   138→                    token_url: ov.token_url.clone(),
   139→                    scopes: ov.scopes.clone(),
   140→                },
   141→            )
   142→        } else {
   143→            McpOAuthProvider::new(name, remote.request_url())
   144→        };
   145→        Arc::new(provider)
   146→    }
   147→
   148→    fn should_attempt_auth_connection(
   149→        has_existing_auth_provider: bool,
   150→        has_oauth_override: bool,
   151→        has_stored_token: bool,
   152→    ) -> bool {
   153→        has_existing_auth_provider || has_oauth_override || has_stored_token
   154→    }
   155→
   156→    /// Start all enabled servers from the registry.
   157→    pub async fn start_enabled(&self) -> Vec<String> {
   158→        let enabled: Vec<(String, McpServerConfig)> = {
   159→            let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   160→            inner
   161→                .registry
   162→                .enabled_servers()
   163→                .into_iter()
   164→                .map(|(name, cfg)| (name.to_string(), cfg.clone()))
   165→                .collect()
   166→        };
   167→
   168→        let mut started = Vec::new();
   169→        for (name, config) in enabled {
   170→            match self.start_server(&name, &config).await {
   171→                Ok(()) => started.push(name),
   172→                Err(e) => warn!(server = %name, error = %e, "failed to start MCP server"),
   173→            }
   174→        }
   175→        started
   176→    }
   177→
   178→    /// Start a single server connection.
   179→    ///
   180→    /// For SSE servers: attempts unauthenticated first. On 401 Unauthorized,
   181→    /// stores auth context and returns `McpManagerError::OAuthRequired`.
   182→    pub async fn start_server(&self, name: &str, config: &McpServerConfig) -> Result<()> {
   183→        // Shut down existing connection if any.
   184→        self.stop_server(name).await;
   185→
   186→        // Network work happens outside the lock.
   187→        let (client, auth_provider) = match config.transport {
   188→            TransportType::Sse | TransportType::StreamableHttp => {
   189→                let env_overrides = {
   190→                    let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   191→                    inner.env_overrides.clone()
   192→                };
   193→                let remote = ResolvedRemoteConfig::from_server_config(config, &env_overrides)
   194→                    .with_context(|| format!("SSE transport for '{name}' requires a url"))?;
   195→
   196→                // Check if we already have an auth provider (from a previous connection).
   197→                let existing_auth = {
   198→                    let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   199→                    inner.auth_providers.get(name).cloned()
   200→                };
   201→
   202→                let has_existing_auth_provider = existing_auth.is_some();
   203→                let auth_provider = existing_auth.unwrap_or_else(|| {
   204→                    Self::build_auth_provider(name, &remote, config.oauth.as_ref())
   205→                });
   206→
   207→                // If we have a stored token, prefer auth transport immediately.
   208→                // This avoids forced re-auth at process start for OAuth-backed servers.
   209→                let has_stored_token = if has_existing_auth_provider {
   210→                    false
   211→                } else {
   212→                    auth_provider.access_token().await?.is_some()
   213→                };
   214→
   215→                if Self::should_attempt_auth_connection(
   216→                    has_existing_auth_provider,
   217→                    config.oauth.is_some(),
   218→                    has_stored_token,
   219→                ) {
   220→                    let client = McpClient::connect_sse_with_auth(
   221→                        name,
   222→                        &remote,
   223→                        auth_provider.clone(),
   224→                        self.effective_timeout_for(config),
   225→                    )
   226→                    .await?;
   227→                    (client, Some(auth_provider))
   228→                } else {
   229→                    // No hint that auth is needed yet, probe unauthenticated first.
   230→                    match McpClient::connect_sse(name, &remote, self.effective_timeout_for(config))
   231→                        .await
   232→                    {
   233→                        Ok(client) => (client, None),
   234→                        Err(e) => {
   235→                            // Check if it's a 401 Unauthorized.
   236→                            if let Error::Transport(McpTransportError::Unauthorized {
   237→                                www_authenticate,
   238→                            }) = &e
   239→                            {
   240→                                info!(
   241→                                    server = %name,
   242→                                    "SSE server requires auth"
   243→                                );
   244→
   245→                                // Mark auth as required and persist challenge metadata.
   246→                                let auth_ok = auth_provider
   247→                                    .handle_unauthorized(www_authenticate.as_deref())
   248→                                    .await?;
   249→
   250→                                if !auth_ok {
   251→                                    let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;
   252→                                    inner.auth_providers.insert(name.to_string(), auth_provider);
   253→                                    return Err(McpManagerError::OAuthRequired {
   254→                                        server: name.to_string(),
   255→                                    }
   256→                                    .into());
   257→                                }
   258→
   259→                                // Retry with auth.
   260→                                let client = McpClient::connect_sse_with_auth(
   261→                                    name,
   262→                                    &remote,
   263→                                    auth_provider.clone(),
   264→                                    self.effective_timeout_for(config),
   265→                                )
   266→                                .await?;
   267→                                (client, Some(auth_provider))
   268→                            } else {
   269→                                return Err(e);
   270→                            }
   271→                        },
   272→                    }
   273→                }
   274→            },
   275→            TransportType::Stdio => {
   276→                let client = McpClient::connect(
   277→                    name,
   278→                    &config.command,
   279→                    &config.args,
   280→                    &config.env,
   281→                    self.effective_timeout_for(config),
   282→                )
   283→                .await?;
   284→                (client, None)
   285→            },
   286→        };
   287→
   288→        // Fetch tools.
   289→        let mut client = client;
   290→        let tool_defs = client.list_tools().await?.to_vec();
   291→        info!(
   292→            server = %name,
   293→            tools = tool_defs.len(),
   294→            "MCP server started with tools"
   295→        );
   296→
   297→        // Atomic insert of client, tools, and auth provider.
   298→        let client: Arc<RwLock<dyn McpClientTrait>> = Arc::new(RwLock::new(client));
   299→        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;
   300→        inner.clients.insert(name.to_string(), client);
   301→        inner.tools.insert(name.to_string(), tool_defs);
   302→
   303→        if let Some(auth) = auth_provider {
   304→            inner.auth_providers.insert(name.to_string(), auth);
   305→        }
   306→
   307→        Ok(())
   308→    }
   309→
   310→    /// Stop a server connection.
   311→    pub async fn stop_server(&self, name: &str) {
   312→        // Atomically remove client and tools, then drop the lock before async shutdown.
   313→        // Keep auth_providers for potential reconnection.
   314→        let client = {
   315→            let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;
   316→            inner.tools.remove(name);
   317→            inner.clients.remove(name)
   318→        };
   319→        if let Some(client) = client {
   320→            let mut c = client.write().await;
   321→            c.shutdown().await;
   322→        }
   323→    }
   324→
   325→    /// Restart a server.
   326→    pub async fn restart_server(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()> {
   327→        let config = {
   328→            let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   329→            inner
   330→                .registry
   331→                .get(name)
   332→                .cloned()
   333→                .with_context(|| format!("MCP server '{name}' not found in registry"))?
   334→        };
   335→        self.start_server(name, &config).await
   336→    }
   337→
   338→    /// Start OAuth for an SSE server and return the browser authorization URL.
   339→    pub async fn oauth_start_server(&self, name: &str, redirect_uri: &str) -> Result<String> {
   340→        let config =
   341→            {
   342→                let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   343→                inner.registry.get(name).cloned().ok_or_else(|| {
   344→                    McpManagerError::ServerNotFound {
   345→                        server: name.to_string(),
   346→                    }
   347→                })?
   348→            };
   349→
   350→        if !matches!(
   351→            config.transport,
   352→            TransportType::Sse | TransportType::StreamableHttp
   353→        ) {
   354→            return Err(McpManagerError::NotRemoteTransport {
   355→                server: name.to_string(),
   356→            }
   357→            .into());
   358→        }
   359→
   360→        let env_overrides = {
   361→            let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   362→            inner.env_overrides.clone()
   363→        };
   364→        let remote = ResolvedRemoteConfig::from_server_config(&config, &env_overrides)?;
   365→
   366→        let existing_auth = {
   367→            let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   368→            inner.auth_providers.get(name).cloned()
   369→        };
   370→        let has_existing_auth_provider = existing_auth.is_some();
   371→        let auth_provider = existing_auth
   372→            .unwrap_or_else(|| Self::build_auth_provider(name, &remote, config.oauth.as_ref()));
   373→
   374→        if !has_existing_auth_provider {
   375→            let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;
   376→            inner
   377→                .auth_providers
   378→                .insert(name.to_string(), auth_provider.clone());
   379→        }
   380→
   381→        auth_provider
   382→            .start_oauth(redirect_uri, None)
   383→            .await?
   384→            .with_context(|| format!("MCP server '{name}' does not support OAuth"))
   385→    }
   386→
   387→    /// Complete an OAuth callback by matching state across MCP auth providers.
   388→    ///
   389→    /// Returns the server name whose OAuth flow was completed.
   390→    pub async fn oauth_complete_callback(&self, state: &str, code: &str) -> Result<String> {
   391→        let providers: Vec<(String, SharedAuthProvider)> = {
   392→            let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   393→            inner
   394→                .auth_providers
   395→                .iter()
   396→                .map(|(name, provider)| (name.clone(), provider.clone()))
   397→                .collect()
   398→        };
   399→
   400→        for (name, provider) in providers {
   401→            if provider.complete_oauth(state, code).await? {
   402→                self.restart_server(&name).await?;
   403→                return Ok(name);
   404→            }
   405→        }
   406→
   407→        Err(McpManagerError::OAuthStateNotFound.into())
   408→    }
   409→
   410→    /// Trigger re-authentication for an SSE server.
   411→    pub async fn reauth_server(&self, name: &str, redirect_uri: &str) -> Result<String> {
   412→        self.oauth_start_server(name, redirect_uri).await
   413→    }
   414→
   415→    /// Get the status of all configured servers.
   416→    pub async fn status_all(&self) -> Vec<ServerStatus> {
   417→        let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   418→
   419→        let mut statuses = Vec::new();
   420→        for (name, config) in &inner.registry.servers {
   421→            let state = if let Some(client) = inner.clients.get(name) {
   422→                let c = client.read().await;
   423→                match c.state() {
   424→                    McpClientState::Ready => {
   425→                        if c.is_alive().await {
   426→                            "running"
   427→                        } else {
   428→                            "dead"
   429→                        }
   430→                    },
   431→                    McpClientState::Connected => "connecting",
   432→                    McpClientState::Authenticating => "authenticating",
   433→                    McpClientState::Closed => "stopped",
   434→                }
   435→            } else {
   436→                "stopped"
   437→            };
   438→
   439→            let auth_state = inner.auth_providers.get(name).map(|a| a.auth_state());
   440→            statuses.push(ServerStatus {
   441→                name: name.clone(),
   442→                state: state.into(),
   443→                enabled: config.enabled,
   444→                tool_count: inner.tools.get(name).map_or(0, |t| t.len()),
   445→                server_info: None,
   446→                command: config.command.clone(),
   447→                args: config.args.clone(),
   448→                env: if matches!(
   449→                    config.transport,
   450→                    TransportType::Sse | TransportType::StreamableHttp
   451→                ) {
   452→                    HashMap::new()
   453→                } else {
   454→                    config.env.clone()
   455→                },
   456→                request_timeout_secs: config.request_timeout_secs,
   457→                configured_request_timeout_secs: self.effective_timeout_secs_for(config),
   458→                transport: config.transport,
   459→                url: config
   460→                    .url
   461→                    .as_ref()
   462→                    .map(|raw| sanitize_url_for_display(raw.expose_secret())),
   463→                header_names: header_names(&config.headers),
   464→                auth_state,
   465→                auth_url: None,
   466→                display_name: config.display_name.clone(),
   467→            });
   468→        }
   469→        statuses
   470→    }
   471→
   472→    /// Get the status of a single server.
   473→    pub async fn status(&self, name: &str) -> Option<ServerStatus> {
   474→        self.status_all().await.into_iter().find(|s| s.name == name)
   475→    }
   476→
   477→    /// Get tool bridges for all running servers (for registration into ToolRegistry).
   478→    pub async fn tool_bridges(&self) -> Vec<McpToolBridge> {
   479→        let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   480→        let mut bridges = Vec::new();
   481→
   482→        for (name, client) in inner.clients.iter() {
   483→            if let Some(tool_defs) = inner.tools.get(name) {
   484→                bridges.extend(McpToolBridge::from_client(
   485→                    name,
   486→                    tool_defs,
   487→                    Arc::clone(client),
   488→                ));
   489→            }
   490→        }
   491→
   492→        bridges
   493→    }
   494→
   495→    /// Get tools for a specific server.
   496→    pub async fn server_tools(&self, name: &str) -> Option<Vec<McpToolDef>> {
   497→        self.inner.read().await.tools.get(name).cloned()
   498→    }
   499→
   500→    // ── Registry operations ─────────────────────────────────────────
   501→
   502→    /// Add a server to the registry and optionally start it.
   503→    pub async fn add_server(
   504→        &self,
   505→        name: String,
   506→        config: McpServerConfig,
   507→        start: bool,
   508→    ) -> Result<()> {
   509→        let enabled = config.enabled;
   510→        {
   511→            let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;
   512→            inner.registry.add(name.clone(), config.clone())?;
   513→        }
   514→        if start && enabled {
   515→            self.start_server(&name, &config).await?;
   516→        }
   517→        Ok(())
   518→    }
   519→
   520→    /// Remove a server from the registry and stop it.
   521→    pub async fn remove_server(&self, name: &str) -> Result<bool> {
   522→        self.stop_server(name).await;
   523→        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;
   524→        inner.auth_providers.remove(name);
   525→        inner.registry.remove(name)
   526→    }
   527→
   528→    /// Enable a server and start it.
   529→    pub async fn enable_server(&self, name: &str) -> Result<bool> {
   530→        let config = {
   531→            let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;
   532→            if !inner.registry.enable(name)? {
   533→                return Ok(false);
   534→            }
   535→            inner.registry.get(name).cloned()
   536→        };
   537→        if let Some(config) = config {
   538→            self.start_server(name, &config).await?;
   539→        }
   540→        Ok(true)
   541→    }
   542→
   543→    /// Disable a server and stop it.
   544→    pub async fn disable_server(&self, name: &str) -> Result<bool> {
   545→        self.stop_server(name).await;
   546→        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;
   547→        inner.registry.disable(name)
   548→    }
   549→
   550→    /// Get a snapshot of the registry for serialization.
   551→    pub async fn registry_snapshot(&self) -> McpRegistry {
   552→        self.inner.read().await.registry.clone()
   553→    }
   554→
   555→    /// Update a server's configuration and restart it if running.
   556→    pub async fn update_server(&self, name: &str, config: McpServerConfig) -> Result<()> {
   557→        let was_running = {
   558→            let inner = self.inner.read().await;
   559→            inner.clients.contains_key(name)
   560→        };
   561→        {
   562→            let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;
   563→            let enabled = inner.registry.get(name).is_none_or(|c| c.enabled);
   564→            let mut new_config = config;
   565→            new_config.enabled = enabled;
   566→            inner.registry.add(name.to_string(), new_config)?;
   567→        }
   568→        if was_running {
   569→            self.restart_server(name).await?;
   570→        }
   571→        Ok(())
   572→    }
   573→
   574→    /// Shut down all servers.
   575→    pub async fn shutdown_all(&self) {
   576→        let names: Vec<String> = self.inner.read().await.clients.keys().cloned().collect();
   577→        for name in names {
   578→            self.stop_server(&name).await;
   579→        }
   580→    }
   581→}

     1→//! HTTP/SSE transport for remote MCP servers (Streamable HTTP transport).
     2→//!
     3→//! Uses HTTP POST for JSON-RPC requests and GET for server-initiated SSE events.
     4→//! Supports optional OAuth Bearer token injection and automatic 401 retry.
     5→
     6→use std::{
     7→    sync::{
     8→        Arc,
     9→        atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},
    10→    },
    11→    time::Duration,
    12→};
    13→
    14→use {
    15→    reqwest::{Client, header::HeaderMap},
    16→    secrecy::{ExposeSecret, Secret},
    17→    tokio::sync::RwLock,
    18→    tracing::{debug, info, warn},
    19→};
    20→
    21→use crate::{
    22→    auth::SharedAuthProvider,
    23→    error::{Context, Error, Result},
    24→    remote::{ResolvedRemoteConfig, sanitize_reqwest_error},
    25→    traits::McpTransport,
    26→    types::{
    27→        JsonRpcNotification, JsonRpcRequest, JsonRpcResponse, McpTransportError, PROTOCOL_VERSION,
    28→    },
    29→};
    30→
    31→const MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION_HEADER: &str = "MCP-Protocol-Version";
    32→const MCP_SESSION_ID_HEADER: &str = "Mcp-Session-Id";
    33→const STREAMABLE_ACCEPT_HEADER: &str = "application/json, text/event-stream";
    34→
    35→/// HTTP/SSE-based transport for a remote MCP server.
    36→pub struct SseTransport {
    37→    client: Client,
    38→    request_url: Secret<String>,
    39→    display_url: String,
    40→    default_headers: HeaderMap,
    41→    next_id: AtomicU64,
    42→    /// Optional auth provider for Bearer token injection.
    43→    auth: Option<SharedAuthProvider>,
    44→    /// Session identifier used by streamable HTTP servers.
    45→    session_id: RwLock<Option<String>>,
    46→}
    47→
    48→impl SseTransport {
    49→    /// Create a new SSE transport pointing at the given MCP server URL.
    50→    pub fn new(url: &str) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
    51→        Self::new_with_timeout(url, Duration::from_secs(60))
    52→    }
    53→
    54→    /// Create a new SSE transport with a custom request timeout.
    55→    pub fn new_with_timeout(url: &str, request_timeout: Duration) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
    56→        let remote = ResolvedRemoteConfig::from_server_config(
    57→            &crate::registry::McpServerConfig {
    58→                transport: crate::registry::TransportType::Sse,
    59→                url: Some(Secret::new(url.to_string())),
    60→                ..Default::default()
    61→            },
    62→            &std::collections::HashMap::new(),
    63→        )?;
    64→        Self::new_with_remote(remote, request_timeout)
    65→    }
    66→
    67→    pub fn new_with_remote(
    68→        remote: ResolvedRemoteConfig,
    69→        request_timeout: Duration,
    70→    ) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
    71→        let client = Client::builder()
    72→            .timeout(request_timeout)
    73→            .build()
    74→            .context("failed to build HTTP client for SSE transport")?;
    75→
    76→        Ok(Arc::new(Self {
    77→            client,
    78→            request_url: Secret::new(remote.request_url().to_string()),
    79→            display_url: remote.display_url().to_string(),
    80→            default_headers: remote.headers().clone(),
    81→            next_id: AtomicU64::new(1),
    82→            auth: None,
    83→            session_id: RwLock::new(None),
    84→        }))
    85→    }
    86→
    87→    /// Create a new SSE transport with an OAuth auth provider.
    88→    pub fn with_auth(url: &str, auth: SharedAuthProvider) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
    89→        Self::with_auth_and_timeout(url, auth, Duration::from_secs(60))
    90→    }
    91→
    92→    /// Create a new SSE transport with an OAuth auth provider and custom request timeout.
    93→    pub fn with_auth_and_timeout(
    94→        url: &str,
    95→        auth: SharedAuthProvider,
    96→        request_timeout: Duration,
    97→    ) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
    98→        let remote = ResolvedRemoteConfig::from_server_config(
    99→            &crate::registry::McpServerConfig {
   100→                transport: crate::registry::TransportType::Sse,
   101→                url: Some(Secret::new(url.to_string())),
   102→                ..Default::default()
   103→            },
   104→            &std::collections::HashMap::new(),
   105→        )?;
   106→        Self::with_auth_remote(remote, auth, request_timeout)
   107→    }
   108→
   109→    pub fn with_auth_remote(
   110→        remote: ResolvedRemoteConfig,
   111→        auth: SharedAuthProvider,
   112→        request_timeout: Duration,
   113→    ) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
   114→        let client = Client::builder()
   115→            .timeout(request_timeout)
   116→            .build()
   117→            .context("failed to build HTTP client for SSE transport")?;
   118→
   119→        Ok(Arc::new(Self {
   120→            client,
   121→            request_url: Secret::new(remote.request_url().to_string()),
   122→            display_url: remote.display_url().to_string(),
   123→            default_headers: remote.headers().clone(),
   124→            next_id: AtomicU64::new(1),
   125→            auth: Some(auth),
   126→            session_id: RwLock::new(None),
   127→        }))
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    /// Build a request builder with optional Bearer token.
   131→    async fn build_post(&self) -> Result<reqwest::RequestBuilder> {
   132→        let mut req = self
   133→            .client
   134→            .post(self.request_url.expose_secret())
   135→            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
   136→            .header("Accept", STREAMABLE_ACCEPT_HEADER)
   137→            .header(MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION_HEADER, PROTOCOL_VERSION);
   138→
   139→        req = self.apply_default_headers(req);
   140→
   141→        if let Some(session_id) = self.session_id.read().await.clone() {
   142→            req = req.header(MCP_SESSION_ID_HEADER, session_id);
   143→        }
   144→
   145→        if let Some(token) = match &self.auth {
   146→            Some(auth) => auth.access_token().await?,
   147→            None => None,
   148→        } {
   149→            req = req.header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", token.expose_secret()));
   150→        }
   151→
   152→        Ok(req)
   153→    }
   154→
   155→    async fn store_session_id_from_response(&self, response: &reqwest::Response) {
   156→        let Some(raw) = response.headers().get(MCP_SESSION_ID_HEADER) else {
   157→            return;
   158→        };
   159→        let Ok(session_id) = raw.to_str() else {
   160→            return;
   161→        };
   162→        if session_id.trim().is_empty() {
   163→            return;
   164→        }
   165→
   166→        let mut slot = self.session_id.write().await;
   167→        let session_id = session_id.to_string();
   168→        if slot.as_ref() != Some(&session_id) {
   169→            debug!(
   170→                url = %self.display_url,
   171→                session_id = %session_id,
   172→                "updated MCP streamable HTTP session id"
   173→            );
   174→            *slot = Some(session_id);
   175→        }
   176→    }
   177→
   178→    fn apply_default_headers(&self, mut req: reqwest::RequestBuilder) -> reqwest::RequestBuilder {
   179→        for (name, value) in &self.default_headers {
   180→            if self.auth.is_some() && name == reqwest::header::AUTHORIZATION {
   181→                continue;
   182→            }
   183→            req = req.header(name, value);
   184→        }
   185→        req
   186→    }
   187→
   188→    fn response_is_event_stream(resp: &reqwest::Response) -> bool {
   189→        resp.headers()
   190→            .get(reqwest::header::CONTENT_TYPE)
   191→            .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
   192→            .map(|ct| {
   193→                ct.split(';')
   194→                    .next()
   195→                    .is_some_and(|base| base.trim() == "text/event-stream")
   196→            })
   197→            .unwrap_or(false)
   198→    }
   199→
   200→    fn parse_event_stream_response(body: &str, method: &str) -> Result<JsonRpcResponse> {
   201→        let mut data = String::new();
   202→
   203→        for line in body.lines() {
   204→            let trimmed = line.trim_end();
   205→            if let Some(rest) = trimmed.strip_prefix("data:") {
   206→                if !data.is_empty() {
   207→                    data.push('\n');
   208→                }
   209→                data.push_str(rest.trim_start());
   210→                continue;
   211→            }
   212→
   213→            if trimmed.is_empty() && !data.is_empty() {
   214→                if let Ok(resp) = serde_json::from_str::<JsonRpcResponse>(&data) {
   215→                    return Ok(resp);
   216→                }
   217→                data.clear();
   218→            }
   219→        }
   220→
   221→        if !data.is_empty()
   222→            && let Ok(resp) = serde_json::from_str::<JsonRpcResponse>(&data)
   223→        {
   224→            return Ok(resp);
   225→        }
   226→
   227→        Err(Error::message(format!(
   228→            "failed to parse JSON-RPC response from event stream for '{method}'"
   229→        )))
   230→    }
   231→
   232→    /// Send a POST request and handle 401 with auth retry.
   233→    /// Returns the HTTP response or a typed `McpTransportError`.
   234→    async fn send_with_auth_retry(
   235→        &self,
   236→        method: &str,
   237→        body: &impl serde::Serialize,
   238→    ) -> Result<reqwest::Response> {
   239→        // First attempt
   240→        let req = self.build_post().await?;
   241→        let http_resp = req
   242→            .json(body)
   243→            .send()
   244→            .await
   245→            .map_err(sanitize_reqwest_error)
   246→            .with_context(|| format!("SSE POST to '{}' for '{method}' failed", self.display_url))?;
   247→
   248→        if http_resp.status() == reqwest::StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED {
   249→            let has_session_header = http_resp.headers().contains_key(MCP_SESSION_ID_HEADER);
   250→            self.store_session_id_from_response(&http_resp).await;
   251→
   252→            if let Some(auth) = &self.auth {
   253→                let first_www_auth = http_resp
   254→                    .headers()
   255→                    .get("www-authenticate")
   256→                    .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
   257→                    .map(String::from);
   258→
   259→                // Some streamable HTTP servers issue a session ID alongside 401
   260→                // and expect subsequent requests to include it. Retry once with
   261→                // current auth/session before forcing interactive re-auth.
   262→                if has_session_header {
   263→                    info!(
   264→                        method = %method,
   265→                        url = %self.display_url,
   266→                        www_authenticate = ?first_www_auth,
   267→                        "received 401 with session header, replaying request before OAuth re-auth"
   268→                    );
   269→
   270→                    let req = self.build_post().await?;
   271→                    let replay_resp = req
   272→                        .json(body)
   273→                        .send()
   274→                        .await
   275→                        .map_err(sanitize_reqwest_error)
   276→                        .with_context(|| {
   277→                            format!(
   278→                                "SSE POST session replay to '{}' for '{method}' failed",
   279→                                self.display_url
   280→                            )
   281→                        })?;
   282→
   283→                    if replay_resp.status() != reqwest::StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED {
   284→                        self.store_session_id_from_response(&replay_resp).await;
   285→                        return Ok(replay_resp);
   286→                    }
   287→
   288→                    self.store_session_id_from_response(&replay_resp).await;
   289→                    let replay_www_auth = replay_resp
   290→                        .headers()
   291→                        .get("www-authenticate")
   292→                        .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
   293→                        .map(String::from);
   294→
   295→                    info!(
   296→                        method = %method,
   297→                        url = %self.display_url,
   298→                        "received 401 after session replay, attempting OAuth re-auth"
   299→                    );
   300→
   301→                    if auth.handle_unauthorized(replay_www_auth.as_deref()).await? {
   302→                        // Retry with new token
   303→                        let req = self.build_post().await?;
   304→                        let retry_resp = req
   305→                            .json(body)
   306→                            .send()
   307→                            .await
   308→                            .map_err(sanitize_reqwest_error)
   309→                            .with_context(|| {
   310→                                format!(
   311→                                    "SSE POST retry to '{}' for '{method}' failed",
   312→                                    self.display_url
   313→                                )
   314→                            })?;
   315→
   316→                        if retry_resp.status() == reqwest::StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED {
   317→                            return Err(McpTransportError::Unauthorized {
   318→                                www_authenticate: retry_resp
   319→                                    .headers()
   320→                                    .get("www-authenticate")
   321→                                    .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
   322→                                    .map(String::from),
   323→                            }
   324→                            .into());
   325→                        }
   326→
   327→                        self.store_session_id_from_response(&retry_resp).await;
   328→                        return Ok(retry_resp);
   329→                    }
   330→
   331→                    return Err(McpTransportError::Unauthorized {
   332→                        www_authenticate: replay_www_auth,
   333→                    }
   334→                    .into());
   335→                }
   336→
   337→                info!(
   338→                    method = %method,
   339→                    url = %self.display_url,
   340→                    www_authenticate = ?first_www_auth,
   341→                    "received 401, attempting OAuth re-auth"
   342→                );
   343→
   344→                if auth.handle_unauthorized(first_www_auth.as_deref()).await? {
   345→                    // Retry with new token
   346→                    let req = self.build_post().await?;
   347→                    let retry_resp = req
   348→                        .json(body)
   349→                        .send()
   350→                        .await
   351→                        .map_err(sanitize_reqwest_error)
   352→                        .with_context(|| {
   353→                            format!(
   354→                                "SSE POST retry to '{}' for '{method}' failed",
   355→                                self.display_url
   356→                            )
   357→                        })?;
   358→
   359→                    if retry_resp.status() == reqwest::StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED {
   360→                        return Err(McpTransportError::Unauthorized {
   361→                            www_authenticate: retry_resp
   362→                                .headers()
   363→                                .get("www-authenticate")
   364→                                .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
   365→                                .map(String::from),
   366→                        }
   367→                        .into());
   368→                    }
   369→
   370→                    self.store_session_id_from_response(&retry_resp).await;
   371→                    return Ok(retry_resp);
   372→                }
   373→            }
   374→
   375→            // No auth provider or auth failed
   376→            return Err(McpTransportError::Unauthorized {
   377→                www_authenticate: http_resp
   378→                    .headers()
   379→                    .get("www-authenticate")
   380→                    .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
   381→                    .map(String::from),
   382→            }
   383→            .into());
   384→        }
   385→
   386→        self.store_session_id_from_response(&http_resp).await;
   387→        Ok(http_resp)
   388→    }
   389→}
   390→
   391→#[async_trait::async_trait]
   392→impl McpTransport for SseTransport {
   393→    async fn request(
   394→        &self,
   395→        method: &str,
   396→        params: Option<serde_json::Value>,
   397→    ) -> Result<JsonRpcResponse> {
   398→        let id = self.next_id.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
   399→        let req = JsonRpcRequest::new(id, method, params);
   400→
   401→        debug!(method = %method, id = %id, url = %self.display_url, "SSE client -> server");
   402→
   403→        let http_resp = self.send_with_auth_retry(method, &req).await?;
   404→
   405→        if !http_resp.status().is_success() {
   406→            let status = http_resp.status();
   407→            let body = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   408→            return Err(Error::message(format!(
   409→                "MCP SSE server returned HTTP {status} for '{method}': {body}"
   410→            )));
   411→        }
   412→
   413→        let resp: JsonRpcResponse = if Self::response_is_event_stream(&http_resp) {
   414→            let body = http_resp
   415→                .text()
   416→                .await
   417→                .with_context(|| format!("failed to read event stream response for '{method}'"))?;
   418→            Self::parse_event_stream_response(&body, method)?
   419→        } else {
   420→            http_resp
   421→                .json()
   422→                .await
   423→                .with_context(|| format!("failed to parse JSON-RPC response for '{method}'"))?
   424→        };
   425→
   426→        if let Some(ref err) = resp.error {
   427→            return Err(Error::message(format!(
   428→                "MCP SSE error on '{method}': code={} message={}",
   429→                err.code, err.message
   430→            )));
   431→        }
   432→
   433→        Ok(resp)
   434→    }
   435→
   436→    async fn notify(&self, method: &str, params: Option<serde_json::Value>) -> Result<()> {
   437→        let notif = JsonRpcNotification {
   438→            jsonrpc: "2.0".into(),
   439→            method: method.into(),
   440→            params,
   441→        };
   442→
   443→        debug!(
   444→            method = %method,
   445→            url = %self.display_url,
   446→            "SSE client -> server (notification)"
   447→        );
   448→
   449→        let http_resp = self.send_with_auth_retry(method, &notif).await?;
   450→
   451→        if !http_resp.status().is_success() {
   452→            let status = http_resp.status();
   453→            warn!(method = %method, %status, "SSE notification returned non-success");
   454→        }
   455→
   456→        Ok(())
   457→    }
   458→
   459→    async fn is_alive(&self) -> bool {
   460→        // Try a lightweight GET request to check connectivity and session continuity.
   461→        let mut req = self
   462→            .client
   463→            .get(self.request_url.expose_secret())
   464→            .timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
   465→            .header("Accept", STREAMABLE_ACCEPT_HEADER)
   466→            .header(MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION_HEADER, PROTOCOL_VERSION);
   467→
   468→        req = self.apply_default_headers(req);
   469→
   470→        if let Some(session_id) = self.session_id.read().await.clone() {
   471→            req = req.header(MCP_SESSION_ID_HEADER, session_id);
   472→        }
   473→
   474→        // Include auth header in health checks too
   475→        if let Some(token) = match &self.auth {
   476→            Some(auth) => auth.access_token().await.ok().flatten(),
   477→            None => None,
   478→        } {
   479→            req = req.header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", token.expose_secret()));
   480→        }
   481→
   482→        match req.send().await {
   483→            Ok(resp) => {
   484→                self.store_session_id_from_response(&resp).await;
   485→                if resp.status() == reqwest::StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED {
   486→                    return true;
   487→                }
   488→                // Any successful response means the server is reachable —
   489→                // Streamable HTTP servers may reply with application/json
   490→                // rather than text/event-stream, which is equally valid.
   491→                resp.status().is_success()
   492→            },
   493→            Err(_) => false,
   494→        }
   495→    }
   496→
   497→    async fn kill(&self) {
   498→        let session_id = {
   499→            let mut slot = self.session_id.write().await;
   500→            slot.take()
   501→        };
   502→
   503→        let Some(session_id) = session_id else {
   504→            return;
   505→        };
   506→
   507→        let mut req = self
   508→            .client
   509→            .delete(self.request_url.expose_secret())
   510→            .timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
   511→            .header(MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION_HEADER, PROTOCOL_VERSION)
   512→            .header(MCP_SESSION_ID_HEADER, session_id);
   513→
   514→        req = self.apply_default_headers(req);
   515→
   516→        if let Some(token) = match &self.auth {
   517→            Some(auth) => auth.access_token().await.ok().flatten(),
   518→            None => None,
   519→        } {
   520→            req = req.header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", token.expose_secret()));
   521→        }
   522→
   523→        if let Err(e) = req.send().await {
   524→            warn!(
   525→                url = %self.display_url,
   526→                error = %sanitize_reqwest_error(e),
   527→                "failed to close MCP streamable HTTP session"
   528→            );
   529→        }
   530→    }
   531→}

   620→    fn remote_with_headers(
   621→        url: &str,
   622→        headers: &[(&str, &str)],
   623→    ) -> crate::remote::ResolvedRemoteConfig {
   624→        let config = crate::registry::McpServerConfig {
   625→            transport: crate::registry::TransportType::Sse,
   626→            url: Some(Secret::new(url.to_string())),
   627→            headers: headers
   628→                .iter()
   629→                .map(|(name, value)| ((*name).to_string(), Secret::new((*value).to_string())))
   630→                .collect(),
   631→            ..Default::default()
   632→        };
   633→        crate::remote::ResolvedRemoteConfig::from_server_config(
   634→            &config,
   635→            &std::collections::HashMap::new(),
   636→        )
   637→        .unwrap()
   638→    }

   808→    #[tokio::test]
   809→    async fn test_sse_transport_401_with_session_replays_before_reauth() {
   810→        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
   811→
   812→        use secrecy::Secret;
   813→
   814→        use crate::auth::{McpAuthProvider, McpAuthState};
   815→
   816→        struct CountingAuthProvider {
   817→            reauth_calls: AtomicUsize,
   818→        }
   819→
   820→        #[async_trait::async_trait]
   821→        impl McpAuthProvider for CountingAuthProvider {
   822→            async fn access_token(&self) -> Result<Option<Secret<String>>> {
   823→                Ok(Some(Secret::new("token-123".to_string())))
   824→            }
   825→
   826→            async fn handle_unauthorized(&self, _www_authenticate: Option<&str>) -> Result<bool> {
   827→                self.reauth_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
   828→                Ok(false)
   829→            }
   830→
   831→            async fn start_oauth(
   832→                &self,
   833→                _redirect_uri: &str,
   834→                _www_authenticate: Option<&str>,
   835→            ) -> Result<Option<String>> {
   836→                Ok(None)
   837→            }
   838→
   839→            async fn complete_oauth(&self, _state: &str, _code: &str) -> Result<bool> {
   840→                Ok(false)
   841→            }
   842→
   843→            fn pending_auth_url(&self) -> Option<String> {
   844→                None
   845→            }
   846→
   847→            fn auth_state(&self) -> McpAuthState {
   848→                McpAuthState::Authenticated
   849→            }
   850→        }
   851→
   852→        let mut server = mockito::Server::new_async().await;
   853→
   854→        let first = server
   855→            .mock("POST", "/")
   856→            .match_header("authorization", "Bearer token-123")
